Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the cellular object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 661. The moduli space has dimension at most 428 with 1 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to cellular theory, we decompose the problem into 661 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 428. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
